
update_kizuna = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable_list = kon_kizuna_list
        }
        clear_variable_list = kon_kizuna_list
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable_list = band_kizuna_list
        }
        clear_variable_list = band_kizuna_list
    }
	 
		tot_sync_band_hq_investment_flags = yes
		tot_sync_band_hq_route_bonuses = yes
		tot_assign_unbound_band_hq_slots_effect = yes
		tot_ensure_default_story_band_members_effect = yes
		update_kon_kizuna = yes
		update_band_kizuna = yes
		update_tot_kizuna_groups = yes
		update_tot_kizuna_profession_specialty_groups = yes
		update_tot_kizuna_faction_default_modifiers = yes
		tot_sync_investment_buy_orders = yes
		tot_band_recalculate_reward_points_total = yes
}

tot_band_clear_annex_inherit_temp_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_source_slot } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_source_slot }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_name } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_name }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_progress } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_progress }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creation_progress } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_creation_progress }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_reward_points } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_reward_points }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_food } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_food }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_entertainment } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_entertainment }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_equipment } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_equipment }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_1_claimed } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_1_claimed }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_2_claimed } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_2_claimed }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_3_claimed } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_3_claimed }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_4_claimed } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_4_claimed }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_5_claimed } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_5_claimed }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_selected_role } remove_variable = tot_band_inherit_selected_role }
}

tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_1_effect = {
	tot_band_clear_annex_inherit_temp_effect = yes
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_source_slot value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_name } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_name value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_name } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_creative_progress value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_creative_progress } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_creation_progress value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_creation_progress } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_reward_points value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_invest_food } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_invest_food value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_invest_food } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_invest_entertainment } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_invest_entertainment value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_invest_entertainment } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_invest_equipment } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_invest_equipment value = scope:tot_annexed_country.var:tot_band_1_invest_equipment } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_creative_midpoint_done value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_tier_1_claimed } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_tier_1_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_tier_2_claimed } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_tier_2_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_tier_3_claimed } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_tier_3_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_tier_4_claimed } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_tier_4_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_tier_5_claimed } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_inherit_tier_5_claimed value = 1 } }
}

	set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_created value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_name } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_name value = var:tot_band_inherit_name } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_progress } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = var:tot_band_inherit_creative_progress } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creation_progress } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress value = var:tot_band_inherit_creation_progress } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_reward_points } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_reward_points value = var:tot_band_inherit_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_food } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_invest_food value = var:tot_band_inherit_invest_food } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_entertainment } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_invest_entertainment value = var:tot_band_inherit_invest_entertainment } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_invest_equipment } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_invest_equipment value = var:tot_band_inherit_invest_equipment } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_creative_midpoint_done } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_1_claimed } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_tier_1_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_2_claimed } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_tier_2_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_3_claimed } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_tier_3_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_4_claimed } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_tier_4_claimed value = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_inherit_tier_5_claimed } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_tier_5_claimed value = 1 } }
	scope:tot_annexed_country ?= {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot var:tot_band_hq_slot = var:tot_band_inherit_source_slot }
			set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_candidate_filter } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_candidate_filter value = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_selected_role } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_selected_role value = 1 } }
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = var:tot_band_inherit_source_slot }
			set_variable = { name = tot_band_id value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_1_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_2_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_3_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_4_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_5_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_6_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_6_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_7_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_7_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_8_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_9_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_9_effect = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_10_created } } tot_band_apply_prepared_annexed_band_to_slot_10_effect = yes }
	tot_band_clear_annex_inherit_temp_effect = yes
}

tot_band_inherit_annexed_country_effect = {
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_1_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_1_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_2_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_2_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_3_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_3_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_4_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_4_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_5_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_5_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_6_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_6_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_7_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_7_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_8_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_8_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_9_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_9_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { exists = scope:tot_annexed_country scope:tot_annexed_country ?= { has_variable = tot_band_10_created } } tot_band_prepare_annexed_band_10_effect = yes tot_band_place_prepared_annexed_band_effect = yes }
	tot_band_clear_annex_inherit_temp_effect = yes
	update_kizuna = yes
}

tot_assign_unbound_band_hq_slots_effect = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter }
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_candidate_filter } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_candidate_filter value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_selected_role } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_selected_role value = 1 } }
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_limit } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created } }
				change_variable = { name = tot_band_limit add = 1 }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 1 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 2 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 2 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 2 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 3 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 3 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 3 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 4 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 4 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 4 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 5 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 5 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 5 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_6_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 6 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 6 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 6 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_7_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 7 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 7 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 7 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 8 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 8 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 8 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_9_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 9 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 9 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 9 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_10_created } }
				if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 10 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 10 } }
				set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_created value = 1 }
				prev = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 10 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

tot_band_recalculate_reward_points_total = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_reward_points_spent_total } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_spent_total value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_1_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_2_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_2_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_3_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_3_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_4_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_4_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_5_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_5_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_6_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_6_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_7_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_7_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_8_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_8_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_9_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_9_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_10_reward_points } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_10_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_debug_reward_points_bonus } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_debug_reward_points_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_reward_points_spent_total } change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = -var:tot_band_reward_points_spent_total } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_reward_points_total < 0 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total value = 0 } }
}

		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 owner = { has_variable = tot_band_1_invest_equipment } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_invest_musical_instrument value = var:tot_band_1_invest_equipment } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_invest_paper value = var:tot_band_1_invest_equipment } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 2 owner = { has_variable = tot_band_2_invest_food } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_invest_groceries value = var:tot_band_2_invest_food } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_invest_tea value = var:tot_band_2_invest_food } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_invest_coffee value = var:tot_band_2_invest_food } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_invest_luxury_foods value = var:tot_band_2_invest_food } } }

tot_gourmet_weekly_update = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_progress } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress value = 0 }
	}
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			is_character_alive = yes
			has_trait = tot_group_sheet_038_trait
		}
		root = {
			change_variable = {
				name = tot_gourmet_progress
				add = 3
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_grain market.mg:grain = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_meat market.mg:meat = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fish market.mg:fish = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_fruit market.mg:fruit = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_groceries market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_tea market.mg:tea = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_coffee market.mg:coffee = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_drinks market.mg:luxury_drinks = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_invest_luxury_foods market.mg:luxury_foods = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 50 } }
		change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 5 }
	}

	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 2 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 4 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 5 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 6 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 8 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { tot_gourmet_variety_count_value >= 9 } change_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_progress add = 3 } }

	tot_gourmet_check_rewards = yes
	update_kizuna = yes
}

tot_gourmet_check_rewards = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:tot_gourmet_progress >= 3500
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_tier_1_claimed }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_tier_1_claimed value = 1 }
		add_modifier = tot_gourmet_reward_tier_1_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:tot_gourmet_progress >= 8500
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_tier_2_claimed }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_tier_2_claimed value = 1 }
		add_modifier = tot_gourmet_reward_tier_2_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:tot_gourmet_progress >= 14000
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_tier_3_claimed }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_tier_3_claimed value = 1 }
		add_modifier = tot_gourmet_reward_tier_3_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:tot_gourmet_progress >= 36000
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_gourmet_tier_4_claimed }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_gourmet_tier_4_claimed value = 1 }
		add_modifier = tot_gourmet_reward_tier_4_modifier
	}
}

update_kon_kizuna = {
	if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					#is_in_government = yes
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait
						}
					}
				}
				leader = {
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = kon_kizuna_list
							target = prev
						}
						
					}
				}
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_in_government = yes
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait
							has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait
						}
					}
				}
				leader = {
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = kon_kizuna_list
							target = prev
						}
						
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 1 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 2 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 3 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 4 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 5 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { kon_kizuna_number == 6 }
			je:je_kizuna_gui = {
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_1
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
				remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
				add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
			}
		}
}
update_band_kizuna = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_limit } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 1 }
	}

	if = { limit = { has_variable_list = band_kizuna_list } clear_variable_list = band_kizuna_list }

	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot
			any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter }
		}
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 2 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_2_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 3 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_3_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 4 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_4_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 5 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_5_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 6 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_6_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 7 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_7_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 8 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_8_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 9 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_9_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 10 } every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } owner = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_10_hq_buildings target = prev } } } }
	}

	every_scope_character = {
		limit = { is_character_alive = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tot_band_id
				OR = {
					has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
					has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
					has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
					has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
					has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
				}
			}
			root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = band_kizuna_list target = prev } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id var:tot_band_id = 1 }
			root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_members target = prev } }
root = { change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_member_count add = 1 } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist } root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_vocalists target = prev } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist } root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_guitarists target = prev } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist } root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_bassists target = prev } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist } root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_keyboardists target = prev } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer } root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_drummers target = prev } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist } root = { change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_vocalist_count add = 1 } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_bassist } root = { change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_bassist_count add = 1 } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist } root = { change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_keyboardist_count add = 1 } } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_role_drummer } root = { change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_drummer_count add = 1 } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { root = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					root = {
						any_scope_state = {
							has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot
							var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_candidate_filter }
								var:tot_band_candidate_filter = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
				root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = tot_band_1_candidates target = prev } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					root = { any_scope_state = { has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 has_variable = tot_band_candidate_filter var:tot_band_candidate_filter = 1 } }
					OR = {
						has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait
						has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait
						has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait
						has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait
						has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Ran_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Moca_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Himari_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Tsugumi_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Tomoe_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Kokoro_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Kaoru_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Hagumi_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Kanon_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Misaki_trait
						has_trait = tot_Kurata_Mashiro_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Kasumi_trait
						has_trait = tot_Bang_Tae_trait

tot_clear_band_role_flags_effect = {
	remove_trait = tot_is_guitarist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_bassist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_keyboardist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_drummer_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_lead_vocalist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_vocalist
}

tot_clear_band_instrument_flags_effect = {
	remove_trait = tot_is_guitarist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_bassist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_keyboardist_trait
	remove_trait = tot_is_drummer_trait
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_guitarist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_bassist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_keyboardist
	remove_variable = tot_band_role_drummer
}
tot_clear_band_member_effect = {
	tot_clear_band_role_flags_effect = yes
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_slot_index } remove_variable = tot_band_slot_index }
	remove_variable = tot_band_id
}

tot_prepare_band_1_member_effect = {
	scope:tot_band_member ?= {
		if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_id NOT = { var:tot_band_id = 1 } } tot_clear_band_role_flags_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_id value = 1 }
	}
}

tot_add_band_vocalist_role_effect = {
	scope:tot_band_member ?= {
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_role_vocalist value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait } add_trait = tot_is_supper_lead_vocalist_trait }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_trait = tot_lead_vocalist_trait } } add_trait = tot_is_lead_vocalist_trait }
	}
}

tot_add_band_guitarist_role_effect = {
	scope:tot_band_member ?= {
		tot_clear_band_instrument_flags_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_role_guitarist value = 1 }
		add_trait = tot_is_guitarist_trait
	}
}

tot_add_band_bassist_role_effect = {
	scope:tot_band_member ?= {
		tot_clear_band_instrument_flags_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_role_bassist value = 1 }
		add_trait = tot_is_bassist_trait
	}

tot_band_add_creative_progress_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta value = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_3_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_4_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_5_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_6_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_6_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_7_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_9_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_9_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_10_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta } }
	remove_variable = tot_band_pending_event_creative_delta
}

tot_band_clear_creative_progress_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_2_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 has_variable = tot_band_3_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_3_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 has_variable = tot_band_4_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_4_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 has_variable = tot_band_5_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_5_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 has_variable = tot_band_6_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_6_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 has_variable = tot_band_7_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_7_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 has_variable = tot_band_8_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_8_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 has_variable = tot_band_9_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_9_creative_midpoint_done }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 has_variable = tot_band_10_creative_midpoint_done } remove_variable = tot_band_10_creative_midpoint_done }
}

tot_band_refund_creative_progress_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_refund } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_refund value = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_refund } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress value = 0 } }

	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_refund } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creative_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_refund } }

tot_band_add_creation_experience_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain value = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_1_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_2_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_3_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_3_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_4_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_4_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_5_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_5_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_6_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_6_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_6_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_7_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_7_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_7_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_8_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_8_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_9_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_9_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_9_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_10_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creation_progress add = var:tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain } tot_band_10_check_rewards = yes }
	remove_variable = tot_band_pending_event_exp_gain
}

tot_band_add_reward_points_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points value = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_reward_points_total } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total value = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_2_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_3_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_3_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_4_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_4_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_5_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_5_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_6_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_6_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_7_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_7_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_8_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_8_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_9_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_9_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_10_reward_points } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_reward_points value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_10_reward_points add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points } }
	change_variable = { name = tot_band_reward_points_total add = var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points }
	remove_variable = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points
}

tot_band_sync_current_pending_route_bonuses = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_2_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 has_variable = tot_band_3_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_3_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 has_variable = tot_band_4_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_4_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 has_variable = tot_band_5_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_5_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 has_variable = tot_band_6_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_6_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 has_variable = tot_band_7_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_7_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 has_variable = tot_band_8_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_8_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 has_variable = tot_band_9_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_9_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 has_variable = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus value = var:tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 1 has_variable = tot_band_1_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_1_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 2 has_variable = tot_band_2_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_2_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 3 has_variable = tot_band_3_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_3_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 4 has_variable = tot_band_4_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_4_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 5 has_variable = tot_band_5_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_5_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 6 has_variable = tot_band_6_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_6_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 7 has_variable = tot_band_7_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_7_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 8 has_variable = tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 9 has_variable = tot_band_9_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_9_reward_point_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_band_id var:tot_band_pending_event_band_id = 10 has_variable = tot_band_10_reward_point_bonus } set_variable = { name = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus value = var:tot_band_10_reward_point_bonus } }
}

tot_band_apply_reward_point_bonus_effect = {
	tot_band_sync_current_pending_route_bonuses = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points
			var:tot_band_pending_event_reward_points > 0
			has_variable = tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus
			var:tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus > 0
		}
		change_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_reward_points add = var:tot_band_current_reward_point_bonus }
	}
}

tot_band_resolve_creative_event = {
	tot_band_sync_current_pending_route_bonuses = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus var:tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus >= 15 }
		random_list = {
			25 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.202 popup = yes } }
			65 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.203 popup = yes } }
			10 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.204 popup = yes } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus var:tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus >= 10 }
		random_list = {
			25 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.202 popup = yes } }
			60 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.203 popup = yes } }
			15 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.204 popup = yes } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus var:tot_band_current_creative_success_bonus >= 5 }
		random_list = {
			25 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.202 popup = yes } }
			55 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.203 popup = yes } }
			20 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.204 popup = yes } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			25 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.202 popup = yes } }
			50 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.203 popup = yes } }
			25 = { trigger_event = { id = kizuna.204 popup = yes } }
		}
	}
}

tot_sync_band_hq_route_bonuses = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_2_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_3_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_3_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_4_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_4_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_5_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_5_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_6_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_6_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_7_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_7_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_8_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_8_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_9_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_9_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_1_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_2_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_2_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_3_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_3_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_4_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_4_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_5_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_5_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_6_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_6_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_7_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_7_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_9_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_9_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_10_reward_point_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_10_reward_point_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_1_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_2_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_2_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_3_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_3_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_4_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_4_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_5_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_5_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_6_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_6_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_7_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_7_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_8_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_8_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_9_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_9_weekly_bonus }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_10_weekly_bonus } remove_variable = tot_band_10_weekly_bonus }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_3_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_4_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_5_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_6_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_7_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_9_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_weekly_bonus value = 0 }
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_hq_slot any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_1 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus value = 5 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_2 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus value = 10 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_3 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_success_bonus value = 15 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 1 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_independent_1 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_reward_point_bonus value = 1 } } }

		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 8 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_independent_1 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus value = 1 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 8 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_independent_2 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_8_reward_point_bonus value = 1 } } }

		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 10 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_1 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus value = 5 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 10 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_2 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus value = 10 } } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_hq_slot = 10 any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter has_active_production_method = pm_band_axis_professional_3 } } owner = { set_variable = { name = tot_band_10_creative_success_bonus value = 15 } } }

tot_band_process_creative_event_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created var:tot_band_1_creative_progress >= 1000 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_weekly_event_locked } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_band_id value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_weekly_event_locked value = 1 }
		trigger_event = { id = kizuna.201 popup = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = tot_band_1_created var:tot_band_1_creative_progress >= 400 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done } NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_weekly_event_locked } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_pending_event_band_id value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_midpoint_done value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_weekly_event_locked value = 1 }
		tot_band_trigger_midpoint_event = yes
	}
}

tot_setup_default_kon_band_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = c:BYZ
			NOT = { has_variable = tot_kon_band_startup_done }
		}
		set_variable = { name = tot_kon_band_startup_done value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_limit } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit < 1 } set_variable = { name = tot_band_limit value = 1 } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_created value = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_name value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_band_headquarter }
			}
			set_variable = { name = tot_band_hq_slot value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_candidate_filter } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_candidate_filter value = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_selected_role } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_selected_role value = 1 } }
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_trait = tot_Kon_Yui_trait }
			save_scope_as = tot_band_member
			tot_appoint_band_1_vocalist_effect = yes
			tot_appoint_band_1_guitarist_effect = yes
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_trait = tot_Kon_Mio_trait }
			save_scope_as = tot_band_member
			tot_appoint_band_1_bassist_effect = yes
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_trait = tot_Kon_Ritsu_trait }
			save_scope_as = tot_band_member
			tot_appoint_band_1_drummer_effect = yes
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_trait = tot_Kon_Tsumugi_trait }
			save_scope_as = tot_band_member
			tot_appoint_band_1_keyboardist_effect = yes
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = { is_character_alive = yes has_trait = tot_Kon_Azusa_trait }
			save_scope_as = tot_band_member
			tot_appoint_band_1_guitarist_effect = yes
		}
		update_kizuna = yes
	}
}

add_kon_kizuna_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:kon_kizuna_number = 2
		}
		add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:kon_kizuna_number = 3
		}
		add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:kon_kizuna_number = 4
		}
		add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:kon_kizuna_number = 5
		}
		add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:kon_kizuna_number = 6
		}
		add_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_6
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = kon_kizuna_modifier_5
	}
}

tot_band_check_rewards = {
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 } tot_band_1_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 2 } tot_band_2_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 3 } tot_band_3_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 4 } tot_band_4_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 5 } tot_band_5_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 6 } tot_band_6_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 7 } tot_band_7_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 8 } tot_band_8_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 9 } tot_band_9_check_rewards = yes }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 10 } tot_band_10_check_rewards = yes }
}

tot_band_initialize_axes = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_axis_interest_commerce } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_axis_interest_commerce value = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_axis_club_professional } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_axis_club_professional value = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_axis_independent_corporate } }
		set_variable = { name = tot_band_axis_independent_corporate value = 100 }
	}
}

# BEGIN GENERATED TOT BAND PER BAND
tot_band_weekly_update = {
	tot_band_initialize_axes = yes
	tot_sync_band_hq_investment_flags = yes
	tot_sync_band_hq_route_bonuses = yes
	if = { limit = { has_variable = tot_band_weekly_event_locked } remove_variable = tot_band_weekly_event_locked }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_1_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creative_progress add = band_1_kizuna_creation_weekly_gain_value } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 1 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_1_creation_progress add = band_1_kizuna_creation_weekly_gain_value } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creative_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 2 NOT = { has_variable = tot_band_2_creation_progress } } set_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creation_progress value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { var:tot_band_limit >= 2 } change_variable = { name = tot_band_2_creative_progress add = band_2_kizuna_creation_weekly_gain_value } }
